+
+**Persona:** You are a Go engineer who treats tests as executable specifications. You write tests to constrain behavior, not to hit coverage targets.
+
+**Thinking mode:** Reason as thoroughly as possible for test strategy design and failure analysis — shallow reasoning misses edge cases and produces brittle tests that pass today but break tomorrow. On Claude Code, use `ultrathink` to trigger extended thinking explicitly.
+
+**Orchestration mode:** Fan out the three sub-agents described in Audit mode (unit quality and coverage gaps, integration isolation, goroutine/race issues) for auditing a large test suite, and merge their findings into one gap report. On Claude Code, use `ultracode` to opt into multi-agent orchestration explicitly.
+
+**Modes:**
+
+- **Write mode** — generating new tests for existing or new code. Work sequentially through the code under test; use `gotests` to scaffold table-driven tests, then enrich with edge cases and error paths.
+- **Review mode** — reviewing a PR's test changes. Focus on the diff: check coverage of new behaviour, assertion quality, table-driven structure, and absence of flakiness patterns. Sequential.
+- **Audit mode** — auditing an existing test suite for gaps, flakiness, or bad patterns (order-dependent tests, missing `t.Parallel()`, implementation-detail coupling). Launch up to 3 parallel sub-agents split by concern: (1) unit test quality and coverage gaps, (2) integration test isolation and build tags, (3) goroutine leaks and race conditions.
+- **Debug mode** — a test is failing or flaky. Work sequentially: reproduce reliably, isolate the failing assertion, trace the root cause in production code or test setup.
+
+> **Community default.** A company skill that explicitly supersedes `samber/cc-skills-golang@golang-testing` skill takes precedence.
+
+**Dependencies:**
+
+- gotests: `go install github.com/cweill/gotests/gotests@latest`
+
+# Go Testing Best Practices
+
+This skill guides the creation of production-ready tests for Go applications. Follow these principles to write maintainable, fast, and reliable tests.
+
+## Best Practices Summary
+
+1. Table-driven tests MUST use named subtests -- every test case needs a `name` field passed to `t.Run`
+2. Integration tests MUST use build tags (`//go:build integration`) to separate from unit tests
+3. Tests MUST NOT depend on execution order -- each test MUST be independently runnable
+4. Independent tests SHOULD use `t.Parallel()` when possible
+5. Tests MUST assert observable behavior and public API contracts, not implementation details -- a test coupled to internals turns every refactor into a test rewrite while proving nothing about the contract
+6. Packages with goroutines SHOULD use `goleak.VerifyTestMain` in `TestMain` to detect goroutine leaks
+7. Use testify as helpers, not a replacement for standard library
+8. Mock interfaces, not concrete types
+9. Keep unit tests fast (< 1ms), use build tags for integration tests
+10. Run tests with race detection in CI
+11. Include examples as executable documentation
+12. Test files MUST be named after the source file under test, not after the function or method being tested
+13. Test functions SHOULD appear in the same order as the functions/methods they test in the source file
+
+## Test Structure and Organization
+
+### File Conventions
+
+```go
+// package_test.go - tests in same package (white-box, access unexported)
+package mypackage
+
+// mypackage_test.go - tests in test package (black-box, public API only)
+package mypackage_test
+```
+
+Name the test file after the source file it tests, not after the function or method under test. Go's convention is one test file per source file (`foo.go` -> `foo_test.go`), because tools (`go test`, coverage reports, IDE "jump to test" navigation, `gotests`) and reviewers all resolve tests by source file, not by symbol. A source file usually declares several functions/methods; splitting its tests by symbol name scatters them across many files and breaks that file-to-file mapping.
+
+```
+// ✓ Good — one test file per source file
+helloworld.go       -> helloworld_test.go   // contains TestHelloWorld, TestAbcd, TestXyz, ...
+
+// ✗ Bad — test file named after the function/method instead of the source file
+helloworld.go       -> abcd_test.go         // wrong: should be helloworld_test.go
+```
+
+Exception: very large source files MAY be split into multiple `_test.go` files by concern (e.g. `foo_test.go` + `foo_edgecases_test.go`), but each split file's name MUST still be derived from the source file name, never from an individual function name. Prefer keeping a single `_test.go` file per source file even when it grows large — splitting adds navigation overhead and is rarely worth it; reach for the exception only when a single file becomes genuinely unwieldy to browse or review.
+
+Within a test file, order test functions to match the order their tested functions/methods appear in the source file. A reader (human or agent) scrolling `foo.go` alongside `foo_test.go` can then find the matching test by position instead of searching; drift between the two orderings compounds every time either file grows.
+
+### Naming Conventions
+
+```go
+func TestAdd(t *testing.T) { ... }               // function test
+func TestMyStruct_MyMethod(t *testing.T) { ... } // method test
+func BenchmarkAdd(b *testing.B) { ... }          // benchmark
+func ExampleAdd() { ... }                        // example
+func FuzzAdd(f *testing.F) { ... }               // fuzz test
+```
+
+## Table-Driven Tests
+
+Table-driven tests are the idiomatic Go way to test multiple scenarios. Always name each test case.
+
+```go
+func TestCalculatePrice(t *testing.T) {
+    tests := []struct {
+        name     string
+        quantity int
+        unitPrice float64
+        expected  float64
+    }{
+        {
+            name:      "single item",
+            quantity:  1,
+            unitPrice: 10.0,
+            expected:  10.0,
+        },
+        {
+            name:      "bulk discount - 100 items",
+            quantity:  100,
+            unitPrice: 10.0,
+            expected:  900.0, // 10% discount
+        },
+        {
+            name:      "zero quantity",
+            quantity:  0,
+            unitPrice: 10.0,
+            expected:  0.0,
+        },
+    }
+
+    for _, tt := range tests {
+        t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
+            got := CalculatePrice(tt.quantity, tt.unitPrice)
+            if got != tt.expected {
+                t.Errorf("CalculatePrice(%d, %.2f) = %.2f, want %.2f",
+                    tt.quantity, tt.unitPrice, got, tt.expected)
+            }
+        })
+    }
+}
+```
+
+## Common Pitfall: Assert Scope Leaking into Subtests
+
+Never create a testify `assert`/`require` instance in the parent test function and reuse it inside `t.Run` closures. `assert.New(t)` captures the exact `*testing.T` it was built with, so if that `t` belongs to the parent, every failure raised inside the subtest gets attributed to the _parent_ test in `go test` output — the failing subtest itself still reports `--- PASS`, silently hiding which case broke. This happens whether or not the subtest calls `t.Parallel()`.
+
+```go
+// WRONG -- `is` is bound to the parent's t
+func TestCalculatePrice(t *testing.T) {
+    is := assert.New(t)
+    for _, tt := range tests {
+        t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
+            is.Equal(tt.expected, CalculatePrice(tt.quantity, tt.unitPrice)) // misattributed on failure
+        })
+    }
+}
+
+// RIGHT -- each subtest builds its own instance from its own t
+func TestCalculatePrice(t *testing.T) {
+    for _, tt := range tests {
+        t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
+            is := assert.New(t)
+            is.Equal(tt.expected, CalculatePrice(tt.quantity, tt.unitPrice))
+        })
+    }
+}
+```
+
+Verify with a deliberately-broken case: if `go test -v -run TestName` shows `--- FAIL: TestName` but every `--- PASS: TestName/subtest_name` line still says PASS, the assert scope is leaking.
+
+## Unit Tests
+
+Unit tests should be fast (< 1ms), isolated (no external dependencies), and deterministic.
+
+## Testing HTTP Handlers
+
+Use `httptest` for handler tests with table-driven patterns. See [HTTP Testing](./references/http-testing.md) for examples with request/response bodies, query parameters, headers, and status code assertions.
+
+## Goroutine Leak Detection with goleak
+
+Use `go.uber.org/goleak` to detect leaking goroutines, especially for concurrent code:
+
+```go
+import (
+    "testing"
+    "go.uber.org/goleak"
+)
+
+

+## Tests added
+
+- `TestAdminResetAuditCountPendingAndAppliedReplay/pending`
+- `TestAdminResetAuditCountPendingAndAppliedReplay/applied`
+
+The test extends the existing `resetAuditTx` fixture in `internal/platform/credentialreset/audit_test.go`. It invokes the real `Service.StageOperation` and `Service.PromoteOperation` through a bound transaction fake, generates and stores a staged authority, and reloads the same authority on replay. It asserts exactly one audit INSERT with action `user.reset_secret` and user target 7 after staging; promotion and replay of the same OperationKey must leave the count at one. The applied subtest also replays promotion.
+
+The fake records every audit INSERT without deduplicating. These are service-level write-count assertions, not proof of PostgreSQL commit/rollback durability. No real DB or integration tag is needed for these tests; this package has no existing DB integration fixture. The handler integration file's header now names this test and `TestAuditFailureCannotLeavePromotableStage`, and explicitly qualifies both coverage limits.
+

+package credentialreset
+
+import (
+	"context"
+	"encoding/json"
+	"errors"
+	"fmt"
+	"strings"
+	"testing"
+
+	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

+	"github.com/onesyue/yueboard/internal/platform/tokenvault"

+type resetAuditTx struct {
+	pgx.Tx
+	auditExists bool
+	lookupErr   error
+	err         error
+	args        []any
+	writes      []string
+	hash        []byte
+}
+
+func (tx *resetAuditTx) Exec(_ context.Context, sql string, args ...any) (pgconn.CommandTag, error) {
+	tx.writes = append(tx.writes, sql)
+	if strings.Contains(sql, "INSERT INTO admin_audit_logs") {
+		tx.args = args
+		return pgconn.CommandTag{}, tx.err
+	}
+	return pgconn.NewCommandTag("INSERT 0 1"), nil
+}
+
+type resetAuditRow func(...any) error
+
+func (r resetAuditRow) Scan(dst ...any) error { return r(dst...) }
+func (tx *resetAuditTx) QueryRow(_ context.Context, sql string, _ ...any) pgx.Row {
+	return resetAuditRow(func(dst ...any) error {
+		if strings.Contains(sql, "FROM admin_audit_logs") {
+			*dst[0].(*bool) = tx.auditExists
+			return tx.lookupErr
+		}
+		if strings.Contains(sql, "request_sha256") {
+			*dst[0].(*[]byte) = tx.hash
+			*dst[1].(*string) = "pending"
+		} else {
+			*dst[0].(*int64) = 7
+		}
+		return nil
+	})
+}
+
+func TestResetAuditMetadataAndFailure(t *testing.T) {
+	for _, scope := range []string{"admin_reset_secret", "yueops_reset_subscription", "user_reset_security", "daily_traffic_reset"} {
+		t.Run(scope, func(t *testing.T) {
+			key := OperationKey{Scope: scope, ActorKey: "admin:42", OperationID: "test-operation", Reason: "confirmed sharing", Source: "heavy-share"}
+			tx := &resetAuditTx{}
+			if err := recordResetAudit(context.Background(), tx, key, 7); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if tx.args[7] != "user" || tx.args[8] != "7" {
+				t.Fatalf("target: %v", tx.args)
+			}
+			var detail map[string]any
+			if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(tx.args[4].(string)), &detail); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			for k, want := range map[string]any{"actor": key.ActorKey, "user_id": float64(7), "reason": key.Reason, "source": key.Source, "operation_id": key.OperationID} {
+				if detail[k] != want {
+					t.Fatalf("%s=%v want %v", k, detail[k], want)
+				}
+			}
+			if scope == "admin_reset_secret" && tx.args[0] != int64(42) {
+				t.Fatal("admin identity missing")
+			}
+			tx.err = context.DeadlineExceeded
+			err := recordResetAudit(context.Background(), tx, key, 7)
+			if !errors.Is(err, ErrAudit) || errors.Is(err, context.DeadlineExceeded) {
+				t.Fatalf("audit timeout must be an explicit failure: %v", err)
+			}
+		})
+	}
+}
+
+func TestAuditFailureCannotLeavePromotableStage(t *testing.T) {
+	key := OperationKey{Scope: "yueops_reset_subscription", ActorKey: "internal-yueops", OperationID: "2f0e0d0c-0b0a-4908-8706-050403020101"}
+	ids, hash, err := validateOperation(key, []int64{7})
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	tx := &resetAuditTx{err: errors.New("audit insert denied"), hash: hash[:]}
+	service := &Service{tx: tx}
+	var op Operation
+	err = service.stageOperationOnce(context.Background(), key, ids, hash, &op, 0)
+	if !errors.Is(err, ErrAudit) {
+		t.Fatalf("stage swallowed audit error: %v", err)
+	}
+	for _, sql := range tx.writes {
+		if strings.Contains(sql, "INSERT INTO credential_reset_stages") || strings.Contains(sql, "UPDATE users") {
+			t.Fatal("audit failure staged or published credentials")
+		}
+	}
+	if tx.args == nil {
+		t.Fatal("audit was not attempted")
+	}
+}
+
+func TestPendingResetAuditBackfill(t *testing.T) {
+	key := OperationKey{Scope: "yueops_reset_subscription", ActorKey: "internal-yueops", OperationID: "operation"}
+	for _, exists := range []bool{false, true} {
+		tx := &resetAuditTx{auditExists: exists}
+		if err := ensureResetAudit(context.Background(), tx, key, 7); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if (tx.args != nil) == exists {
+			t.Fatalf("exists=%v audit insertion=%v", exists, tx.args != nil)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, tx := range []*resetAuditTx{{lookupErr: context.DeadlineExceeded}, {err: context.DeadlineExceeded}} {
+		err := ensureResetAudit(context.Background(), tx, key, 7)
+		if !errors.Is(err, ErrAudit) || errors.Is(err, context.DeadlineExceeded) {
+			t.Fatalf("failed closed: %v", err)
+		}
+	}
+}

+func TestAdminResetAuditPreservesRequestMetadata(t *testing.T) {
+	key := OperationKey{
+		Scope: "admin_reset_secret", ActorKey: "admin:42",
+		OperationID: "2f0e0d0c-0b0a-4908-8706-050403020101",
+		Method:      "POST", URI: "/api/v2/test-admin/user/resetSecret", IP: "203.0.113.42",
+	}
+	for _, backfill := range []bool{false, true} {
+		t.Run(map[bool]string{false: "staging", true: "pending backfill"}[backfill], func(t *testing.T) {
+			tx := &resetAuditTx{}
+			var err error
+			if backfill {
+				err = ensureResetAudit(context.Background(), tx, key, 7)
+			} else {
+				ids, hash, validationErr := validateOperation(key, []int64{7})
+				if validationErr != nil {
+					t.Fatal(validationErr)
+				}
+				tx.hash = hash[:]
+				// Stop after the audit insert, before generating or storing credentials.
+				service := &Service{tx: tx, entropy: strings.NewReader("")}
+				var op Operation
+				err = service.stageOperationOnce(context.Background(), key, ids, hash, &op, 0)
+				if !errors.Is(err, ErrEntropy) {
+					t.Fatalf("staging did not reach authority generation: %v", err)
+				}
+				err = nil
+			}
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if len(tx.args) != 10 {
+				t.Fatalf("audit insert not reached: %v", tx.args)
+			}
+			if tx.args[2] != key.Method || tx.args[3] != key.URI || tx.args[5] != key.IP {
+				t.Fatalf("request metadata lost: method=%v uri=%v ip=%v", tx.args[2], tx.args[3], tx.args[5])
+			}
+		})
+	}
+	_, originalHash, err := validateOperation(key, []int64{7})
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	key.Method, key.URI, key.IP = "PUT", "/different-route", "2001:db8::42"
+	_, replayHash, err := validateOperation(key, []int64{7})
+	if err != nil || originalHash != replayHash {
+		t.Fatalf("request metadata changed idempotency identity: %v", err)
+	}

+type resetAuditTx struct {
+	pgx.Tx
+	auditExists bool
+	lookupErr   error
+	err         error
+	args        []any
+	writes      []string
+	hash        []byte
+}
+
+func (tx *resetAuditTx) Exec(_ context.Context, sql string, args ...any) (pgconn.CommandTag, error) {
+	tx.writes = append(tx.writes, sql)
+	if strings.Contains(sql, "INSERT INTO admin_audit_logs") {
+		tx.args = args
+		return pgconn.CommandTag{}, tx.err
+	}
+	return pgconn.NewCommandTag("INSERT 0 1"), nil
+}
+
+type resetAuditRow func(...any) error
+
+func (r resetAuditRow) Scan(dst ...any) error { return r(dst...) }
+func (tx *resetAuditTx) QueryRow(_ context.Context, sql string, _ ...any) pgx.Row {
+	return resetAuditRow(func(dst ...any) error {
+		if strings.Contains(sql, "FROM admin_audit_logs") {
+			*dst[0].(*bool) = tx.auditExists
+			return tx.lookupErr
+		}
+		if strings.Contains(sql, "request_sha256") {
+			*dst[0].(*[]byte) = tx.hash
+			*dst[1].(*string) = "pending"
+		} else {
+			*dst[0].(*int64) = 7
+		}
+		return nil
+	})
+}

+func TestResetAuditMetadataAndFailure(t *testing.T) {
+	for _, scope := range []string{"admin_reset_secret", "yueops_reset_subscription", "user_reset_security", "daily_traffic_reset"} {
+		t.Run(scope, func(t *testing.T) {
+			key := OperationKey{Scope: scope, ActorKey: "admin:42", OperationID: "test-operation", Reason: "confirmed sharing", Source: "heavy-share"}
+			tx := &resetAuditTx{}
+			if err := recordResetAudit(context.Background(), tx, key, 7); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if tx.args[7] != "user" || tx.args[8] != "7" {
+				t.Fatalf("target: %v", tx.args)
+			}
+			var detail map[string]any
+			if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(tx.args[4].(string)), &detail); err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			for k, want := range map[string]any{"actor": key.ActorKey, "user_id": float64(7), "reason": key.Reason, "source": key.Source, "operation_id": key.OperationID} {
+				if detail[k] != want {
+					t.Fatalf("%s=%v want %v", k, detail[k], want)
+				}
+			}
+			if scope == "admin_reset_secret" && tx.args[0] != int64(42) {
+				t.Fatal("admin identity missing")
+			}
+			tx.err = context.DeadlineExceeded
+			err := recordResetAudit(context.Background(), tx, key, 7)
+			if !errors.Is(err, ErrAudit) || errors.Is(err, context.DeadlineExceeded) {
+				t.Fatalf("audit timeout must be an explicit failure: %v", err)
+			}
+		})
+	}

+func TestAuditFailureCannotLeavePromotableStage(t *testing.T) {
+	key := OperationKey{Scope: "yueops_reset_subscription", ActorKey: "internal-yueops", OperationID: "2f0e0d0c-0b0a-4908-8706-050403020101"}
+	ids, hash, err := validateOperation(key, []int64{7})
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	tx := &resetAuditTx{err: errors.New("audit insert denied"), hash: hash[:]}
+	service := &Service{tx: tx}
+	var op Operation
+	err = service.stageOperationOnce(context.Background(), key, ids, hash, &op, 0)
+	if !errors.Is(err, ErrAudit) {
+		t.Fatalf("stage swallowed audit error: %v", err)
+	}
+	for _, sql := range tx.writes {
+		if strings.Contains(sql, "INSERT INTO credential_reset_stages") || strings.Contains(sql, "UPDATE users") {
+			t.Fatal("audit failure staged or published credentials")
+		}
+	}
+	if tx.args == nil {
+		t.Fatal("audit was not attempted")
+	}
+}

+func TestPendingResetAuditBackfill(t *testing.T) {
+	key := OperationKey{Scope: "yueops_reset_subscription", ActorKey: "internal-yueops", OperationID: "operation"}
+	for _, exists := range []bool{false, true} {
+		tx := &resetAuditTx{auditExists: exists}
+		if err := ensureResetAudit(context.Background(), tx, key, 7); err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		if (tx.args != nil) == exists {
+			t.Fatalf("exists=%v audit insertion=%v", exists, tx.args != nil)
+		}
+	}
+	for _, tx := range []*resetAuditTx{{lookupErr: context.DeadlineExceeded}, {err: context.DeadlineExceeded}} {
+		err := ensureResetAudit(context.Background(), tx, key, 7)
+		if !errors.Is(err, ErrAudit) || errors.Is(err, context.DeadlineExceeded) {
+			t.Fatalf("failed closed: %v", err)
+		}
+	}

+func TestAdminResetAuditPreservesRequestMetadata(t *testing.T) {
+	key := OperationKey{
+		Scope: "admin_reset_secret", ActorKey: "admin:42",
+		OperationID: "2f0e0d0c-0b0a-4908-8706-050403020101",
+		Method:      "POST", URI: "/api/v2/test-admin/user/resetSecret", IP: "203.0.113.42",
+	}
+	for _, backfill := range []bool{false, true} {
+		t.Run(map[bool]string{false: "staging", true: "pending backfill"}[backfill], func(t *testing.T) {
+			tx := &resetAuditTx{}
+			var err error
+			if backfill {
+				err = ensureResetAudit(context.Background(), tx, key, 7)
+			} else {
+				ids, hash, validationErr := validateOperation(key, []int64{7})
+				if validationErr != nil {
+					t.Fatal(validationErr)
+				}
+				tx.hash = hash[:]
+				// Stop after the audit insert, before generating or storing credentials.
+				service := &Service{tx: tx, entropy: strings.NewReader("")}
+				var op Operation
+				err = service.stageOperationOnce(context.Background(), key, ids, hash, &op, 0)
+				if !errors.Is(err, ErrEntropy) {
+					t.Fatalf("staging did not reach authority generation: %v", err)
+				}
+				err = nil
+			}
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			if len(tx.args) != 10 {
+				t.Fatalf("audit insert not reached: %v", tx.args)
+			}
+			if tx.args[2] != key.Method || tx.args[3] != key.URI || tx.args[5] != key.IP {
+				t.Fatalf("request metadata lost: method=%v uri=%v ip=%v", tx.args[2], tx.args[3], tx.args[5])
+			}
+		})
+	}
+	_, originalHash, err := validateOperation(key, []int64{7})
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	key.Method, key.URI, key.IP = "PUT", "/different-route", "2001:db8::42"
+	_, replayHash, err := validateOperation(key, []int64{7})
+	if err != nil || originalHash != replayHash {
+		t.Fatalf("request metadata changed idempotency identity: %v", err)
+	}
+	return "pending"
+}

+type ApplyFunc func(context.Context, OperationKey, []int64) (ApplyReceipt, error)
+
+type Service struct {
+	tx      pgx.Tx
+	pool    *pgxpool.Pool
+	entropy io.Reader
+}
+
+func New(pool *pgxpool.Pool) *Service {
+	return &Service{pool: pool, entropy: rand.Reader}
+}
+
+// WithTx binds both staging and promotion to an existing business transaction.
+// The caller owns its commit/rollback. Lock errors propagate without retrying
+// inside an already-aborted transaction.
+func (s *Service) WithTx(tx pgx.Tx) *Service {
+	if tx == nil {
+		panic("credentialreset: nil transaction")
+	}
+	clone := *s
+	clone.tx = tx
+	return &clone
+}
+func (s *Service) transaction(ctx context.Context, fn func(pgx.Tx) error) error {
+	if s.tx != nil {
+		return fn(s.tx)
+	}
+	return pgx.BeginTxFunc(ctx, s.pool, pgx.TxOptions{}, fn)
+}
+
+// NewForTest supplies deterministic entropy without a mutable package global.
+func NewForTest(pool *pgxpool.Pool, entropy io.Reader) *Service {
+	if entropy == nil {
+		entropy = rand.Reader
+	}
+	return &Service{pool: pool, entropy: entropy}
+}
+
+// StageOperation durably reserves new authorities without changing users.uuid
+// or users.token. Node snapshots read the pending UUID from the stage table;
+// public readers cannot see it until PromoteOperation publishes it.
+func (s *Service) StageOperation(
+	ctx context.Context,
+	key OperationKey,
+	userIDs []int64,
+) (Operation, error) {
+	if s == nil || s.pool == nil {
+		return Operation{}, errors.New("credential reset store is unavailable")
+	}
+	canonical, requestHash, err := validateOperation(key, userIDs)
+	if err != nil {
+		return Operation{}, err
+	}
+	// Retry the whole transaction on a lock timeout. Losing one race against a
+	// large accounting transaction is ordinary; those commit in ~52ms on
+	// average, so a second attempt almost always wins. Bounded so the worst
+	// case stays far below the caller's own budget.
+	var operation Operation
+	for attempt := 0; ; attempt++ {
+		operation = Operation{}
+		err = s.stageOperationOnce(ctx, key, canonical, requestHash, &operation, 0)
+		if s.tx != nil || !isLockTimeout(err) || attempt >= stageLockRetries {
+			break
+		}
+		select {
+		case <-ctx.Done():
+			return Operation{}, ctx.Err()
+		case <-time.After(time.Duration(attempt+1) * 250 * time.Millisecond):
+		}
+	}
+	if err != nil {
+		if isLockTimeout(err) {
+			return Operation{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: user row is locked by another transaction", ErrOperationBusy)
+		}
+		return Operation{}, err
+	}
+	return operation, nil
+}
+
+// stageLockRetries bounds the retries above. Three attempts with backoff keeps
+// the worst case near 13s -- comfortably inside the reset request's budget.
+const stageLockRetries = 2
+
+func (s *Service) stageOperationOnce(
+	ctx context.Context,
+	key OperationKey,
+	canonical []int64,
+	requestHash [sha256.Size]byte,
+	out *Operation,
+	lockedUserID int64,
+) error {
+	operation := Operation{}
+	err := s.transaction(ctx, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
+		// Bound how long this transaction will queue on a row lock.
+		//
+		// Staging locks the target `users` row. The accounting path locks a
+		// *batch* of user rows per report -- `SELECT id FROM users WHERE
+		// id = ANY($1) ORDER BY id FOR UPDATE`, 2.08M calls with a 19.8s
+		// maximum in production -- so a single-user reset routinely waited
+		// behind one of those. Measured 2026-08-01: a real reset took 44s
+		// end to end, ~19s of it here, which is what made the button feel
+		// broken and previously produced gateway timeouts.
+		//
+		// Without a bound the wait is whatever the busiest accounting
+		// transaction happens to be. With one, a contended attempt fails
+		// fast and is retried by the caller below, which is dramatically
+		// cheaper than holding an HTTP request open.
+		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '4s'`); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		tag, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `
+			INSERT INTO credential_reset_operations
+			  (scope,actor_key,operation_id,request_sha256,state,created_at,updated_at)
+			VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4,'pending',now(),now())
+			ON CONFLICT (scope,actor_key,operation_id) DO NOTHING`,
+			key.Scope, key.ActorKey, key.OperationID, requestHash[:])
+		if err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		operation = Operation{Key: key, Created: tag.RowsAffected() == 1}
+		var storedHash []byte
+		var state string
+		if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `
+			SELECT request_sha256,state
+			FROM credential_reset_operations
+			WHERE scope=$1 AND actor_key=$2 AND operation_id=$3
+			FOR UPDATE`, key.Scope, key.ActorKey, key.OperationID).Scan(&storedHash, &state); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		if !equalDigest(storedHash, requestHash[:]) {
+			return ErrOperationConflict
+		}
+		if !operation.Created {
+			results, err := loadStagesTx(ctx, tx, key)
+			if err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			operation.Results = results
+			operation.Applied = state == "applied"
+			return nil
+		}
+
+		for _, userID := range canonical {
+			if userID != lockedUserID {
+				var found int64
+				if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT id FROM users WHERE id=$1 FOR UPDATE`, userID).Scan(&found); err != nil {
+					if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
+						continue
+					}
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+			// Audit and durable staging commit together. A failed audit must not
+			// leave work that the background promoter could later apply.
+			if err := recordResetAudit(ctx, tx, key, userID); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			result, err := s.newStagedAuthority(ctx, tx, userID)
+			if err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			// 暂存 token 只写密文三列（00096 contract：明文列 next_token 不再写，留 NULL）；
+			// promote 时三列原样搬进 users。未配置 vault 时 Seal 为全 nil——那会铸出一个
+			// 谁也查不到的 token，所以拒绝而不是写进去。
+			sealed, err := tokenvault.Default().Seal(result.Token)
+			if err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if sealed.Bidx == nil {
+				return tokenvault.ErrDisabled
+			}
+			if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `
+				INSERT INTO credential_reset_stages
+				  (scope,actor_key,operation_id,user_id,next_uuid,
+				   next_token_enc,next_token_bidx,next_token_key_id)
+				VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4,$5,$6,$7,$8)`,
+				key.Scope, key.ActorKey, key.OperationID, userID, result.UUID,
+				sealed.Enc, sealed.Bidx, sealed.KeyID); err != nil {
+				if isPendingUserConflict(err) {
+					return ErrPendingOperation
+				}
+				return err
+			}
+			operation.Results = append(operation.Results, result)
+		}
+		return nil
+	})
+	*out = operation
+	return err
+}
+
+// isLockTimeout reports the PostgreSQL lock_not_available condition raised when
+// `SET LOCAL lock_timeout` expires while waiting for a row lock.
+func isLockTimeout(err error) bool {
+	var pgErr *pgconn.PgError
+	return errors.As(err, &pgErr) && pgErr.Code == "55P03"
+}
+
+// PromoteOperation atomically publishes staged authorities. Publication alone
+// does not prove node acknowledgement.
+func (s *Service) PromoteOperation(ctx context.Context, key OperationKey) (Operation, error) {
+	if s == nil || s.pool == nil {
+		return Operation{}, errors.New("credential reset store is unavailable")
+	}
+	if _, _, err := validateOperation(key, []int64{1}); err != nil {
+		return Operation{}, err
+	}
+	var operation Operation
+	err := s.transaction(ctx, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
+		var state string
+		if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `
+			SELECT state FROM credential_reset_operations
+			WHERE scope=$1 AND actor_key=$2 AND operation_id=$3
+			FOR UPDATE`, key.Scope, key.ActorKey, key.OperationID).Scan(&state); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		results, err := loadStagesTx(ctx, tx, key)
+		if err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		operation = Operation{Key: key, Results: results, Applied: state == "applied"}
+		if operation.Applied {
+			return nil
+		}
+		// Pending operations created before audit coverage must also fail closed.
+		// The operation row lock serializes replays, so this cannot duplicate
+		// an audit written by another promoter for the same operation.
+		for _, result := range results {
+			if err := ensureResetAudit(ctx, tx, key, result.UserID); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		if err := promoteStagesTx(ctx, tx, key, results, nil); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		operation.Applied = true
+		return nil
+	})
+	if err != nil {
+		return Operation{}, err
+	}
+	return operation, nil
+}
+
+// StageAndPromoteLockedUser stages and publishes one already locked user in
+// the transaction supplied by WithTx. The caller must hold that exact user's
+// row lock until commit/rollback, and must roll back on any error. No staged
+// state escapes this call or can be supplied by the caller. beforePromote runs
+// once after staging pending work, allowing notices to reflect processing time.
+// It must only derive the promotion context, without mutating database state.
+func (s *Service) StageAndPromoteLockedUser(
+	ctx context.Context,
+	key OperationKey,
+	userID int64,
+	beforePromote func(context.Context) context.Context,
+) (Operation, error) {
+	if s == nil || s.pool == nil || s.tx == nil {
+		return Operation{}, errors.New("credential reset requires a bound transaction")
+	}
+	canonical, requestHash, err := validateOperation(key, []int64{userID})
+	if err != nil {
+		return Operation{}, err
+	}
+	var staged Operation
+	if err := s.stageOperationOnce(ctx, key, canonical, requestHash, &staged, userID); err != nil {
+		if isLockTimeout(err) {
+			return Operation{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: user row is locked by another transaction", ErrOperationBusy)
+		}
+		return Operation{}, err
+	}
+	if staged.Applied {
+		return staged, nil
+	}
+	if beforePromote != nil {
+		ctx = beforePromote(ctx)
+	}
+	if !staged.Created {
+		// Existing operations may contain promoted stages. Preserve the generic
+		// replay path and its unpromoted-only retirement selection.
+		return s.PromoteOperation(ctx, key)
+	}
+	if err := promoteStagesTx(ctx, s.tx, key, staged.Results, canonical); err != nil {
+		return Operation{}, err
+	}
+	return Operation{Key: key, Results: staged.Results, Applied: true}, nil
+}
+
+// freshIDs is private evidence from staging a newly created operation in this
+// transaction. nil retains the generic unpromoted-stage lookup.
+func promoteStagesTx(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, key OperationKey, results []Result, freshIDs []int64) error {
+	// 旧 token 的入口名在换 token **之前**退休：换完就读不到旧 token 了。
+	// 旧订阅文件里的名字本来经桶通配一直解析到池 IP（token 死、入口不死），
+	// 退休表让 YueOps 把它们显式钉到牺牲品——泄漏出去的旧订阅随重置一起失效。
+	var retireErr error
+	if freshIDs == nil {
+		retireErr = retireStagedTokens(ctx, tx, key)
+	} else {
+		retireErr = retirePromotionTokens(ctx, tx, key, freshIDs)
+	}
+	if retireErr != nil {
+		return retireErr
+	}
+	// 00096 contract：只搬密文三列，明文列 users.token 一个字都不碰——下一版的迁移会删
+	// 这一列，而删列执行时本二进制仍在跑，语句里出现列名就会 42703。留在明文列里的旧
+	// 值已经查不到任何人（查人只走 token_bidx），随删列一起消失。
+	if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `
+		UPDATE users AS u
+		SET uuid=s.next_uuid,
+		    token_enc=s.next_token_enc, token_bidx=s.next_token_bidx, token_key_id=s.next_token_key_id,
+		    online_count=0, updated_at=now()
+		FROM credential_reset_stages AS s
+		WHERE s.scope=$1 AND s.actor_key=$2 AND s.operation_id=$3
+		  AND s.promoted_at IS NULL AND u.id=s.user_id`,
+		key.Scope, key.ActorKey, key.OperationID); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `
+		UPDATE credential_reset_stages SET promoted_at=now()
+		WHERE scope=$1 AND actor_key=$2 AND operation_id=$3 AND promoted_at IS NULL`,
+		key.Scope, key.ActorKey, key.OperationID); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	// The Board-owned account inbox uses the same user identity and commits
+	// atomically with the rotation. Never infer a Telegram identity from it.
+	if err := deliverResetMessages(ctx, tx, key, results); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `
+		UPDATE credential_reset_operations
+		SET state='applied',applied_at=now(),updated_at=now()
+		WHERE scope=$1 AND actor_key=$2 AND operation_id=$3`,
+		key.Scope, key.ActorKey, key.OperationID); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	return nil

+## Execution discipline (owner feedback, 2026-10-12: "slow, divergent, repeats work")
+
+Evidence rules above set the *standard*, not the *volume*. Scale verification to
+risk (docs/copy/tests: targeted tests; service code: module suite + the CI lens,
+i.e. the Linux container; money/auth/migrations/prod/fleet: full gate + prod
+read-back). Land a verified batch at least every 90 minutes. Every action maps to a
+checklist item; out-of-scope findings go to a one-line "Parked" list unless P0.
+Reuse evidence already recorded in the round doc for the same object and
+revision; do not re-measure or re-read unchanged material. One lane per question,
+and a darwin-skipped test is not a passed test.
+
+The following owner rules are tracked here so a fresh checkout does not depend
+on a workstation-only `~/.codex/AGENTS.md`:
+
+- Deliver a small, single-topic, verifiable batch within 90 minutes. If blocked,
+  record the blocker and expected resolution in the round log. Ready fixes do
+  not wait for wider audits.
+- Name the checklist item for each action. Only P0 incidents (production outage,
+  data loss or an actively exploited vulnerability) may interrupt the scope.
+- Reuse evidence for the same object and revision. Remeasure only when the
+  object changes, evidence exceeds 24 hours, or evidence conflicts. Do not reread
+  whole files already read in the session. After two identical command failures,
+  investigate the cause or change approach before retrying.
+- Report status changes: what changed, where the evidence is, and what is next.
+  Close an item once its acceptance criteria are met; do not keep polishing it.
+- Use exact-path staging in each independent repository. Do not overwrite
+  another session's work or change an executing script's bytes.
+- Preserve owner-opened interactive sessions. Coordination, takeover, an idle
+  screen or a release-lock conflict does not authorize signaling, closing or
+  injecting input into the owner's Grok, Codex, Claude or terminal session.
+  Stop only a worker this session created, or one explicitly transferred to
+  this session, with a verified receipt (session ID, PID and start time); an
+  owner instruction to stop the specific session also authorizes it. Reading
+  another lane's receipt does not transfer ownership. Otherwise record the
+  conflict in the existing inbox and keep independent work moving. See
+  `yueto-orchestration` for the process-ownership boundary.
+- Repository documentation, including historical audit and release narratives,
+  and explanatory comments use English. Preserve product translations, exact
+  test inputs, protocol values and original evidence identifiers. Reply to the
+  owner in Chinese unless requested otherwise.
+- The sole production execution entry is
+  `docs/2026-10-17-whole-estate-execution-plan.md`, starting at its current r4
+  control section and linked production backlog. Update that plan in place;
+  do not create a competing master plan. The 10-16 focused plan supplies inherited
+  acceptance contracts, not current scheduling. Agent maintenance is recorded in
+  `docs/2026-10-16-agent-maintenance-round.md`; the English backlog remains in
+  `docs/2026-10-16-english-cleanup-round.md` and its existing CSV.
+
+- Owner decision (2026-10-20): backup creation/verification, off-site backup
+  work, PITR/disaster-restore drills, and backup-retirement planning are canceled
+  from current and future work. Do not reintroduce them as tasks, observation
+  waits or admission prerequisites from inherited plans, skills or old findings
+  unless the owner explicitly reverses this decision. See R4.2b in the sole
+  execution plan. Preserve historical evidence. This planning exclusion does
+  not cancel release rollback, interrupted-transaction recovery, live replication
+  or client connection recovery; it does not authorize deleting stored data or
+  changing installed production schedules.

+
+This is the **`yue.to` workspace root** — an umbrella over a family of independent git repos that together make up a privatized (from-Xboard) VPN/proxy service: panel, node fleet, client apps, and ops. It is NOT a monorepo. Each subdirectory is its own repository; the root repo tracks nine directories — `docs/`, `scripts/`, `design/`, `i18n/`, `schedule/`, `help/`, `.claude/` (skills/rules/agents/hooks/settings only; `worktrees/`/`projects/`/`state/` stay ignored), `.agents/` (Codex skill links), `.grok/` (Grok rules, LSP, and three skill overrides; estate skills stay in `.claude/skills/`) — plus `.codex/hooks.json` and a few top-level files: `yueto.code-workspace`, `.gitleaksignore`, and the signed release desired state `release.yaml` + `release.yaml.asc` (`.gitignore` is `/*/` + allowlist, so root *files* are not ignored). Root `reports/` is a local, untracked scratch area — never commit it or cite it as current state. Most real work happens **inside** a component repo, each of which has its own richer `CLAUDE.md` — read that one too when working in it.
+
+## Where the knowledge lives: this file, skills, rules
+
+This file is **always-loaded context** and is kept under the official ~200-line target. Everything
+else has a home that loads on demand — that is the whole design, not a filing convenience:
+
+- **`.claude/skills/` — workspace skills**, symlinked into `~/.claude/skills/` so they load
+  through `.agents/skills/` for Codex and installed for both agents by `scripts/sync-agent-skills.py --apply --user`. Procedural knowledge: worthless most of the time, and must be
+  word-for-word correct the rest of the time.
+- **`.claude/rules/` — path-scoped rules** (`paths:` frontmatter). A rule enters context only when
+  a file it names is read. Use it for "this *file class* has a trap", never for prose.
+- **`.claude/agents/` — project subagents**: `component-worker` (edits a component repo from its own
+  worktree under root `.claude/worktrees/` — never `isolation: worktree`) and `readonly-investigator`.
+
+| Skill (routes by **domain**) | Load it when |
+|---|---|
+| `guard-authoring` | changing/reviewing guard coverage, contract checks, CI assertions or probes; diagnosing test import/fixture leakage; ordinary fixes that only run existing checks do not trigger it |
+| `release-chain` | planning or running any release, image roll, promote or production deploy |
+| `node-lifecycle` | classifying maintenance/retirement or supplier-quota availability; provisioning, cloning, rebuilding or hard-deleting a fleet node |
+| `fleet-diagnose` | diagnosing node/fleet/network/latency/loss/resource problems, **before** changing production |
+| `pg-safety` | writing a Postgres migration, GRANT/role manifest, cross-ownership table access, erasure change, or a spend/eligibility query |
+| `generated-blocks` | changing design tokens, user-facing strings/translations, help articles, or any recurring job |
+| `round-closeout` | closing out an audit / optimization / incident round |
+| `yueto-orchestration` | running an authorized multi-session round under the current coordinator (Claude or Codex): one release exit, side-branch candidates, inbox, release train and shutdown; no fixed-model approval prerequisite |
+
+| Skill (routes by **action**) | Load it when |
+|---|---|
+| `yueto-agent-maintenance` | equipping, configuring or diagnosing coding-agent capabilities for this workspace |
+| `evidence-discipline` | interpreting operational measurements or asserting production/fleet health or runtime improvement; match the claim to its evidence |
+| `landed-not-written` | installing executable scripts/configuration/automation or claiming an installed change took effect; verify the actual consumer |
+| `change-blast-radius` | removing a boundary, renaming, moving a file, zeroing a parameter, dropping a wait, or touching a constant other code pins |
+| `concurrent-git-safety` | committing/pushing, spawning concurrent agents into one repo, or editing anything while a long-running script executes |
+| `patrol` | routine read-only patrol of failed units, DNS sentinel receipts, relay-pool evidence and local workload admission; never changes production |
+| `readonly-reconcile` | read-only balance/points reconciliation or release accounting comparisons; never repairs data |
+| `release-readback` | after a signed release: reconcile promoted, desired and running images, migration receipts and before/after accounting evidence |

+not a recursive reading list. Reuse loaded guidance and read only relevant reference sections.

+func equalDigest(a, b []byte) bool { return bytes.Equal(a, b) }
+
+func loadStagesTx(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, key OperationKey) ([]Result, error) {
+	rows, err := tx.Query(ctx, `
+		SELECT user_id,next_uuid,next_token_enc,next_token_key_id
+		FROM credential_reset_stages
+		WHERE scope=$1 AND actor_key=$2 AND operation_id=$3
+		ORDER BY user_id`, key.Scope, key.ActorKey, key.OperationID)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	defer rows.Close()
+	results := make([]Result, 0)
+	for rows.Next() {
+		var result Result
+		var tokenEnc []byte
+		var tokenKeyID *int16
+		if err := rows.Scan(&result.UserID, &result.UUID, &tokenEnc, &tokenKeyID); err != nil {
+			return nil, err
+		}
+		// 暂存 token 只从密文解出（00096 contract）。
+		result.Token = tokenvault.Default().Reveal(tokenEnc, tokenKeyID)
+		results = append(results, result)
+	}
+	return results, rows.Err()
+}
+
+func (s *Service) newStagedAuthority(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, userID int64) (Result, error) {
+	for range 8 {
+		newUUID, err := uuid.NewRandomFromReader(s.entropy)
+		if err != nil {
+			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: %v", ErrEntropy, err)
+		}
+		var tokenBytes [16]byte
+		if _, err := io.ReadFull(s.entropy, tokenBytes[:]); err != nil {
+			return Result{}, fmt.Errorf("%w: %v", ErrEntropy, err)
+		}
+		result := Result{UserID: userID, UUID: newUUID.String(), Token: hex.EncodeToString(tokenBytes[:])}
+		// 碰撞判据与查人路径同一个谓词（tokenvault.Match，只按盲索引）：bidx = HMAC(token)，
+		// bidx 相等 ⇔ token 相等，所以「被占用」集合与按明文比时相同。
+		vault := tokenvault.Default()
+		usersMatch, usersArgs := vault.Match("token_bidx", result.Token, 2)
+		stagesMatch, stagesArgs := vault.Match("next_token_bidx", result.Token, 2+len(usersArgs))
+		args := append(append([]any{result.UUID}, usersArgs...), stagesArgs...)
+		var available bool
+		if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `
+			SELECT NOT EXISTS (
+				SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE lower(btrim(uuid))=$1 OR `+usersMatch+`
+				UNION ALL
+				SELECT 1 FROM credential_reset_stages
+				 WHERE lower(btrim(next_uuid))=$1 OR `+stagesMatch+`
+			)`, args...).Scan(&available); err != nil {
+			return Result{}, err
+		}
+		if available {
+			return result, nil
+		}
+	}
+	return Result{}, errors.New("credential reset authority collision retry exhausted")
+}
+
+func isPendingUserConflict(err error) bool {
+	var pgErr *pgconn.PgError
+	return errors.As(err, &pgErr) && pgErr.Code == "23505" &&
+		pgErr.ConstraintName == "uq_credential_reset_pending_user"
+}
+
+// retireStagedTokens 把本次操作要换掉的 token 的入口名写进退休表（同事务）。
+func retireStagedTokens(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, key OperationKey) error {
+	rows, err := tx.Query(ctx, `
+		SELECT user_id FROM credential_reset_stages
+		WHERE scope=$1 AND actor_key=$2 AND operation_id=$3 AND promoted_at IS NULL`,
+		key.Scope, key.ActorKey, key.OperationID)
+	if err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	var ids []int64
+	for rows.Next() {
+		var id int64
+		if err := rows.Scan(&id); err != nil {
+			rows.Close()
+			return err
+		}
+		ids = append(ids, id)
+	}
+	rows.Close()
+	if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	return retirePromotionTokens(ctx, tx, key, ids)
+}
+
+func retirePromotionTokens(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, key OperationKey, ids []int64) error {
+	if _, err := entryretire.RetireUsers(ctx, tx, ids, "token-reset:"+key.Scope, time.Now()); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	// 重置 token = 泄漏出去的那份订阅当场作废，隔离已无收益。不清它，对外承诺的
+	// 「本人重置 token 即可恢复」就是一句做不到的话（隔离挂在 users 行上，与 token 无关）。
+	_, err := entryretire.Release(ctx, tx, ids)
+	return err
+}
+
+// recordResetAudit records an accepted reset once, in the staging transaction.
+// Replays do not reset the audit cooldown; credentials are never logged.
+func recordResetAudit(ctx context.Context, tx audit.Execer, key OperationKey, userID int64) error {
+	reason, source := strings.TrimSpace(key.Reason), strings.TrimSpace(key.Source)
+	if reason == "" {
+		reason = key.Scope
+	}
+	if source == "" {
+		source = key.Scope
+	}
+	action := audit.UserResetSubscription
+	var adminID int64
+	if key.Scope == "admin_reset_secret" {
+		action = audit.UserResetSecret
+		adminID, _ = strconv.ParseInt(strings.TrimPrefix(key.ActorKey, "admin:"), 10, 64)
+	}
+	err := audit.Record(ctx, tx, audit.Entry{
+		AdminID: adminID, Action: action, Method: key.Method, URI: key.URI, IP: key.IP, TargetType: "user", TargetID: strconv.FormatInt(userID, 10),
+		Detail: map[string]any{"actor": key.ActorKey, "user_id": userID, "reason": reason,
+			"source": source, "operation_id": key.OperationID, "scope": key.Scope, "activation_state": "pending"},
+	})
+	if err != nil {
+		// Do not let an audit timeout be mistaken for a fleet-pending receipt.
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: %v", ErrAudit, err)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+func ensureResetAudit(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, key OperationKey, userID int64) error {
+	var exists bool
+	err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT EXISTS (
+  SELECT 1 FROM admin_audit_logs
+  WHERE action IN ('user.reset_subscription', 'user.reset_secret')
+   AND target_type='user' AND target_id=$1
+   AND detail->>'scope'=$2 AND detail->>'actor'=$3
+   AND detail->>'operation_id'=$4
+ )`, strconv.FormatInt(userID, 10), key.Scope, key.ActorKey, key.OperationID).Scan(&exists)
+	if err != nil {
+		return fmt.Errorf("%w: %v", ErrAudit, err)
+	}
+	if exists {
+		return nil
+	}
+	return recordResetAudit(ctx, tx, key, userID)
+}

+// adminResetSecretServer injects a controllable reset service so both activation
+// branches can be exercised independently of fleet capability reports.
+func adminResetSecretServer(t *testing.T, apply credentialreset.ApplyFunc) (*httptest.Server, string) {
+	t.Helper()
+	mod := admin.New(testPool, settings.New(testPool), nil, nil)
+	mod.ResetCredentials = apply
+	r := chi.NewRouter()
+	mod.Routes(r)
+	srv := httptest.NewServer(r)
+	t.Cleanup(srv.Close)
+	return srv, "/" + mod.SecurePath()
+}
+
+func adminResetSecretPost(t *testing.T, srv *httptest.Server, prefix string, body map[string]any) int {
+	t.Helper()
+	status, _ := adminResetSecretPostReceipt(t, srv, prefix, body)
+	return status
+}
+
+func adminResetSecretPostReceipt(t *testing.T, srv *httptest.Server, prefix string, body map[string]any) (int, string) {
+	t.Helper()
+	payload, err := json.Marshal(body)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	r, err := http.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, srv.URL+prefix+"/user/resetSecret", bytes.NewReader(payload))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	r.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+adminToken)
+	r.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
+	res, err := http.DefaultClient.Do(r)
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	defer res.Body.Close()
+	var response struct {
+		Data struct {
+			ActivationState string `json:"activation_state"`
+		} `json:"data"`
+	}
+	if err := json.NewDecoder(res.Body).Decode(&response); err != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("decode reset response: %v", err)
+	}
+	return res.StatusCode, response.Data.ActivationState
+}
+
+func assertAdminResetSecretMetadata(t *testing.T, calls <-chan credentialreset.OperationKey, operationID, path, reason string) {
+	t.Helper()
+	var key credentialreset.OperationKey
+	select {
+	case key = <-calls:
+	default:
+		t.Fatal("handler did not call ResetCredentials")
+	}
+	for field, values := range map[string][2]string{
+		"Source":      {key.Source, "admin"},
+		"Scope":       {key.Scope, "admin_reset_secret"},
+		"ActorKey":    {key.ActorKey, "admin:1"}, // adminToken authenticates fixture user 1.
+		"OperationID": {key.OperationID, operationID},
+		"Method":      {key.Method, http.MethodPost},
+		"URI":         {key.URI, path},
+		"Reason":      {key.Reason, reason},
+	} {
+		if values[0] != values[1] {
+			t.Errorf("%s = %q, want %q", field, values[0], values[1])
+		}
+	}
+	if key.IP == "" {
+		t.Error("audit IP is empty")
+	}
+	if len(calls) != 0 {
+		t.Fatal("handler called ResetCredentials more than once")
+	}
+}
+
+// Pending responses must forward the request metadata even before activation.
+func TestAdminResetSecretAuditsPendingBranch(t *testing.T) {
+	operationID := fmt.Sprintf("11111111-1111-4111-8111-%012d", time.Now().UnixNano()%1e12)
+	const reason = "administrator requested credential reset"
+	calls := make(chan credentialreset.OperationKey, 4)
+	srv, prefix := adminResetSecretServer(t, func(
+		_ context.Context, key credentialreset.OperationKey, _ []int64,
+	) (credentialreset.ApplyReceipt, error) {
+		calls <- key
+		return credentialreset.ApplyReceipt{
+			Operation: credentialreset.Operation{Key: key, Created: true},
+		}, credentialreset.ErrApplyPending
+	})
+
+	status, state := adminResetSecretPostReceipt(t, srv, prefix, map[string]any{
+		"id": 2, "operation_id": operationID, "reason": reason,
+	})
+	if status != http.StatusAccepted || state != "pending" {
+		t.Fatalf("pending response = (%d, %q), want (202, pending)", status, state)
+	}
+	assertAdminResetSecretMetadata(t, calls, operationID, prefix+"/user/resetSecret", reason)
+}
+
+// Applied requests forward metadata for both creation and idempotent replay.
+// Audit deduplication belongs to credentialreset, not the handler.
+func TestAdminResetSecretAuditsAppliedBranchIncludingReplay(t *testing.T) {
+	operationID := fmt.Sprintf("22222222-2222-4222-8222-%012d", time.Now().UnixNano()%1e12)
+	const reason = "administrator requested credential reset"
+	calls := make(chan credentialreset.OperationKey, 4)
+	created := true
+	srv, prefix := adminResetSecretServer(t, func(
+		_ context.Context, key credentialreset.OperationKey, _ []int64,
+	) (credentialreset.ApplyReceipt, error) {
+		calls <- key
+		receipt := credentialreset.ApplyReceipt{
+			Operation: credentialreset.Operation{Key: key, Created: created, Applied: true},
+		}
+		created = false // The next request replays the same operation.
+		return receipt, nil
+	})
+
+	for _, name := range []string{"created", "replay"} {
+		t.Run(name, func(t *testing.T) {
+			status, state := adminResetSecretPostReceipt(t, srv, prefix, map[string]any{
+				"id": 2, "operation_id": operationID, "reason": reason,
+			})
+			if status != http.StatusOK || state != "applied" {
+				t.Fatalf("applied response = (%d, %q), want (200, applied)", status, state)
+			}
+			assertAdminResetSecretMetadata(t, calls, operationID, prefix+"/user/resetSecret", reason)
+		})
+	}
+}

+// writeUserBanResult 是批量封禁的响应写出点（黄金夹具 admin-user-ban.golden.json 经它录制）。
+func writeUserBanResult(w http.ResponseWriter, banned int, skipped int64) {
+	apiresp.Success(w, map[string]any{"banned": banned, "skipped": skipped})
+}

+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// resetSecret
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+func (m *Module) userResetSecret(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
+	ctx := r.Context()
+	body := userBody(r)
+	id, ok := userToInt64(body["id"])
+	if !ok {
+		apiresp.Fail(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "用户不存在")
+		return
+	}
+	var exists bool
+	if err := m.pool.QueryRow(ctx, "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE id = $1)", id).Scan(&exists); err != nil {
+		apiresp.ServerError(w, r, err, "查询失败")
+		return
+	}
+	if !exists {
+		apiresp.Fail(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "用户不存在")
+		return
+	}
+	operationID, ok := body["operation_id"].(string)
+	if !ok || m.ResetCredentials == nil {
+		apiresp.Fail(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "operation_id 无效")
+		return
+	}
+	adminID, _ := auth.UserID(ctx)
+	receipt, err := m.ResetCredentials(ctx, credentialreset.OperationKey{
+		Reason: userResetReason(body), Source: "admin",
+		Method: r.Method, URI: r.URL.Path, IP: userClientIP(r),
+		Scope: "admin_reset_secret", ActorKey: "admin:" + strconv.FormatInt(adminID, 10), OperationID: operationID,
+	}, []int64{id})
+	if errors.Is(err, credentialreset.ErrApplyPending) || errors.Is(err, context.DeadlineExceeded) {
+		writeAdminResetReceipt(w, "pending", operationID, id, receipt)
+		return
+	}
+	if errors.Is(err, credentialreset.ErrInvalidOperation) {
+		apiresp.Fail(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "operation_id 无效")
+		return
+	}
+	if errors.Is(err, credentialreset.ErrOperationBusy) {
+		// The row stayed locked by a large accounting transaction. Retryable and
+		// nothing was staged, so say that instead of reporting a failure.
+		apiresp.Fail(w, http.StatusConflict, "凭据正忙，请稍后重试")
+		return
+	}
+	if errors.Is(err, credentialreset.ErrOperationConflict) || errors.Is(err, credentialreset.ErrPendingOperation) {
+		apiresp.Fail(w, http.StatusConflict, "另一项凭据操作正在进行")
+		return
+	}
+	if err != nil {
+		apiresp.Fail(w, http.StatusServiceUnavailable, "凭据同步失败，请重试")
+		return
+	}
+	writeAdminResetReceipt(w, "applied", operationID, id, receipt)
+}

+	if state == "pending" {
+		apiresp.Accepted(w, map[string]any{

+// Init 解析环境变量并装为进程默认实例；配置错误原样返回，由 main 决定退出。
+func Init() (*Vault, error) {
+	v, err := FromEnv()
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	SetDefault(v)
+	return v, nil
+}
+
+// SetDefault 替换进程默认实例，返回恢复函数（测试用；main 经 Init 调它）。
+func SetDefault(v *Vault) (restore func()) {
+	defaultOnce.Do(func() {}) // 之后的 Default() 不再覆盖显式设置
+	defaultMu.Lock()
+	prev := defaultV
+	if v == nil {
+		v = &Vault{}
+	}
+	defaultV = v
+	defaultMu.Unlock()
+	return func() {
+		defaultMu.Lock()
+		defaultV = prev
+		defaultMu.Unlock()
+	}
+}

+// Enabled 报告是否配置了密钥（未配置 = 查不到人、取不到 token）。
+func (v *Vault) Enabled() bool { return v != nil && len(v.aeads) > 0 && len(v.bidxKey) > 0 }

+def baseline(path):
+    def unique(pairs):
+        result = {}
+        for key, value in pairs:
+            if key in result:
+                raise ValueError("duplicate baseline key")
+            result[key] = value
+        return result
+
+    def nonempty(value):
+        return isinstance(value, str) and bool(value.strip())
+
+    contents = path.read_bytes()
+    if hashlib.sha256(contents).hexdigest() != ACCEPTED_HISTORY_SHA256:
+        raise ValueError("baseline SHA256 does not match approved snapshot")
+    value = json.loads(contents, object_pairs_hook=unique)
+    if (not isinstance(value, dict) or set(value) != {"schema", "frozen_at", "approver", "entries"}
+            or type(value["schema"]) is not int or value["schema"] != 1
+            or not nonempty(value["frozen_at"]) or not nonempty(value["approver"])
+            or not isinstance(value["entries"], list)):
+        raise ValueError("invalid baseline schema")
+    if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}"
+                        r"(?:\.[0-9]{1,6})?(?:Z|[+-](?:[01][0-9]|2[0-3]):[0-5][0-9])",
+                        value["frozen_at"]):
+        raise ValueError("baseline frozen_at must be an ISO timezone timestamp")
+    frozen_at = timestamp(value["frozen_at"])
+    result = {}
+    for entry in value["entries"]:
+        if (not isinstance(entry, dict) or set(entry) != {"repo", "pr", "head", "findings", "reason"}
+                or entry["repo"] not in REPOS or type(entry["pr"]) is not int or entry["pr"] <= 0
+                or not isinstance(entry["head"], str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-fA-F]{40}", entry["head"])
+                or not isinstance(entry["findings"], list) or not all(map(nonempty, entry["findings"]))
+                or len(set(entry["findings"])) != len(entry["findings"]) or not nonempty(entry["reason"])):
+            raise ValueError("invalid baseline entry")
+        key = (entry["repo"], entry["pr"], entry["head"])
+        if key in result:
+            raise ValueError("duplicate baseline entry")
+        result[key] = entry
+    return frozen_at, result
+
+

+class AcceptedHistory(unittest.TestCase):
+    def setUp(self):
+        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
+        self.addCleanup(self.tmp.cleanup)
+        self.path = Path(self.tmp.name) / "accepted-history-20261103.json"
+        self.entry = {"repo": "yueops", "pr": 203, "head": "a" * 40,
+                      "findings": ["no_approving_review_at_merge", "required_merge_receipt_missing"],
+                      "reason": "Frozen historical evidence accepted by coordinator"}
+        self.value = {"schema": 1, "frozen_at": "2026-11-03T02:00:00Z",
+                      "approver": "coordinator (owner mandate t454u) 2026-11-03",
+                      "entries": [self.entry]}
+        self.write_baseline()
+        self.row = {"repo": "yueops", "pr": 203, "head": "a" * 40,
+                    "url": "https://github.com/onesyue/yueops/pull/203",
+                    "merged_at": "2026-11-02T10:00:00Z", "findings": self.entry["findings"][:],
+                    "exception": None, "receipt": None, "satisfied_by_receipt": []}
+        self.candidate = self.row | {"pr": 204, "merged_at": None, "findings": [],
+                                     "url": "https://github.com/onesyue/yueops/pull/204"}
+
+    def write_baseline(self, contents=None):
+        self.path.write_text(json.dumps(self.value) if contents is None else contents)
+        self.enterContext(patch.object(AUDIT, "ACCEPTED_HISTORY_SHA256",
+                                       hashlib.sha256(self.path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()))
+
+    def run_cli(self, mode="full", output_format="text", default_path=False):
+        import contextlib
+        import io
+        args = ["audit", "--format", output_format]
+        if not default_path:
+            args += ["--baseline", str(self.path)]
+        if mode == "premerge":
+            args += ["--premerge", self.candidate["repo"], str(self.candidate["pr"])]
+        with patch("sys.argv", args), patch.object(AUDIT, "audit", return_value=[self.row.copy()]), patch.object(
+                AUDIT, "premerge", return_value=self.candidate.copy()), patch.object(
+                AUDIT, "exceptions", return_value={}), contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()) as output:
+            rc = AUDIT.main()
+        return rc, output.getvalue()
+
+    def test_matched_entry_is_info_and_passes_in_both_modes(self):
+        for remaining in (self.entry["findings"], self.entry["findings"][:1]):
+            self.row["findings"] = remaining
+            for mode in ("full", "premerge"):
+                with self.subTest(mode=mode, remaining=remaining):
+                    rc, output = self.run_cli(mode)
+                    self.assertEqual(rc, 0)
+                    self.assertIn(f'INFO accepted-history {self.row["url"]}: {", ".join(remaining)}', output)
+                    self.assertIn(self.entry["reason"], output)
+                    self.assertNotIn("UNREGISTERED", output)
+                    rc, output = self.run_cli(mode, "json")
+                    result = json.loads(output)
+                    self.assertEqual(rc, 0)
+                    self.assertEqual(result["accepted_history"], 1)
+                    self.assertEqual(result["unregistered"], 0)
+
+    def test_head_repo_pr_mismatch_or_extra_finding_is_unregistered(self):

+                       {"findings": self.entry["findings"] + ["check_nonpassing_at_merge:policy"]}):

+    def test_premerge_findings_cannot_be_covered_by_baseline(self):

---
name: evidence-discipline
description: 'Use for Yue.to post-release readback, read-only production accounting reconciliation, routine inspection, or operational health claims. Prove collection reached the target, match scope and freshness, and distinguish healthy, failed and UNKNOWN.'
---

# Evidence discipline (Yue.to)

For post-release readback, read-only accounting reconciliation or routine
inspection, read [Read-only operational evidence](references/readonly-operations.md).
It supplies collection criteria without authorizing production access or repairs.

The most common failure here is a conclusion with **no supporting evidence**
whose output looks identical to an evidenced conclusion. On 2026-08-29 this
recurred independently in five unrelated places, including two newly written
scripts.

Every rule below comes from an observed incident. Use `guard-authoring` to write
guards and `fleet-diagnose` to investigate nodes. This skill governs **the claim
you are about to make**.

## Select the evidence before collecting again

Start with the selected item's latest receipt: object/input revision, environment
(including timezone, shard order and concurrency when relevant), covered behavior,
result, timestamp and evidence path. Reuse matching evidence within 24 hours;
remeasure changed inputs, conflicting results or expired evidence. A changed
runtime/environment changes the object even if the source SHA is unchanged.
Collect only the missing obligation. A targeted test proves that tested behavior;
it does not replace a required full release gate or current production readback.
State the claim at the layer actually observed and close the item once its
acceptance is satisfied. Historical incidents without a denominator establish
failure mechanisms, not an error rate or a measured performance improvement.

## Answer three questions before stating a conclusion

1. **What does this output say when probing fails?** If it looks the same as
   health or failure, it has no evidentiary value.
2. **Did I observe not-X, or merely fail to observe X?** These are different
   propositions.
3. **Did I measure the layer I am making a claim about?** Inbound reachability
   is not usability, ICMP is not the data plane, and cached receipts are not
   liveness.

If any answer is missing, report **UNKNOWN / UNMEASURABLE**, not either verdict.

## 1. Unmeasurable state mistaken for a definite verdict

This causes both **false green** (unreachable becomes healthy) and **false red**
(unreachable becomes failed). Observed forms include:

- SSH fails, collection returns an empty string, and output says HY2 is not
  listening on UDP/443. That is a data-plane claim supported only by inability
  to reach the host.
- An observation point outside China judges whether the Chinese path is blocked.
  That plane cannot observe the proposition, always returns UP and vetoed
  recovery for 9 hours. **Write down what a veto gate can establish first.**
- `dev-null-fleet-guard` reports SEEN from a cached `checked_at` receipt; it does
  not establish liveness.
- A listener assertion after `sleep 15` mistakes a host taking longer than
  15 seconds to bind for a host unable to start. **Poll readiness until it appears**
  within the operation's bound instead of assuming a fixed wait suffices.
- All inbound 443 checks pass while outbound access reaches only the same
  datacenter (`yue-us-premium`). Reachability does not establish usability.

Fallback repair can hide this family completely: "fails every time and gets
repaired" and "never failed" can produce identical observable state. A timer
that repairs the state every N minutes is itself a reason to inspect the path.
Where authorized and operationally appropriate, suspend the fallback for the
measurement so it cannot mask the failure; preserve the recovery path.

For every collect-then-assert path, first prove collection succeeded (nonempty,
expected fields), then inspect contents. Missing measurements are
UNKNOWN/UNREACHABLE. An inspection unable to measure should `exit 75`, not
`exit 1`.

## 2. A negative observation requires proof of handler arrival

A rate-limit test sent 30 fake tokens to `checkin.yue.to/api/client/feedback`
and counted 30 non-429 responses as passes. That hostname actually returned
404: thirty 404s had been mistaken for thirty admitted requests.

"No rate limit/error/alert" and "code was never reached" have identical counts.
Anti-probing decoy 404s can further hide a missing route as bad data.

First obtain a signal **generated by the target handler itself**, such as an
empty-body 400 `content_required`, not a 404 from elsewhere. Then count results.
Run a negative/control comparison too: a blocking result may have blocked
legitimate traffic as well.

## 3. The tool can manufacture evidence

Every example below has contaminated actual conclusions:

| Failure shape | Consequence | Check or remedy |
|---|---|---|
| Read `$?` after `cmd \| tail` | Captures tail's status; four mutation runs falsely passed | Capture `rc=$?` immediately on its own line, with no intervening command, including `echo`; preserve the producer status |
| `echo "rc=$?"` also contains `$(date)` | Command-substitution expansion overwrote status; rc 78 was recorded as 0 | Capture status separately and immediately |
| `ssh -n` with heredoc/`bash -s` | stdin becomes /dev/null; remote reads zero bytes and always exits 0 | Look for `-n` and `<<` together |
| `${...}`/backticks in a remote SSH command | Premature remote-shell expansion produced two opposite fleet conclusions in one day | Include a known-good control host in the same batch |
| zsh `$VAR:path` | Modifier parsing silently rewrites the command; errors name a nonexistent file | Quote the whole value |
| `__pycache__` / Flutter kernel cache | Same-length edits loaded stale bytecode; all four mutations falsely passed | After editing, `sleep 2 && touch`, or clear the relevant cache |
| Playwright `fullPage` stitching | Turned an entire home section white; visual baseline missed three major regions | Quantify pixels rather than trusting visual inspection alone |
| Matching structural fingerprints | Reported green for syntactically invalid YAML | Require successful parsing; shape alone is insufficient |
| Background jobs inherit `SIGINT=SIG_IGN` | Bash cannot install a trap for an already ignored signal | A failure only in the SIGINT parameter is the signature |

## 4. The criterion itself can be wrong

- **Too broad:** `dpkg -l | awk '$1 !~ /^ii/'` treated normal `rc` (removed,
  configuration retained) as failure and reported every host failed after a
  successful rollout. The intended predicate is `^i[^i]`.
- **Too narrow/non-universal:** `ss | grep -c :443` is 2 on a dual-stack host,
  not 1. Establish universality before asserting an exact count.
- **Measuring the probe:** instantaneous single samples, counting the probe
  itself, or self-matching `pgrep -f X`.
- **Wrong method:** binary-search MTU on a lossy path mistakes loss for oversized
  packets. Repeat fixed-size probes and inspect the pattern, not the converged
  search value.
- **Agreement is not correctness:** comparing two handwritten copies detects
  one-sided changes but is blind when both are stale. All four checks passed
  during actual Thailand2/Indonesia2 VLESS disconnections. Ask who detects A
  and B being wrong together; add a runtime-derived observation when necessary.
- **Contaminated sampling:** check for other heavy work in the sample window
  before attributing load. Geodata distribution once caused 5 false positives.
- **Inventory is not forwarding topology (2026-10-08):** do not combine
  `servers/server_nodes` hosts, protocol nodes, relay membership and other
  product pools. Distinguish entry SID, logical node ID and actual backend.
  Read current nginx upstreams, HY2 MAP and loaded nft targets, then reconcile
  native machine presence. One node ID bound to several hosts proves bindings
  only, not multiple current v4 exits in a region or readiness for backend load
  balancing. Measure primary/backup entry and exit redundancy separately.
- **Batch success is not item success (2026-10-08):** a protocol sweep using
  consecutive-failure alerting can `exit 0` with VLESS 49/50. Locate the exact
  invocation output with
  `systemctl show <unit> -p InvocationID -p Result -p ExecMainStatus -p StandardOutput`,
  then verify each protocol's denominator and OK/FAIL/UNKNOWN counts. A later
  96/96 cannot erase the prior failure. Handshake reachability is not bandwidth
  recovery evidence.
- **Queued alerts are not delivered alerts (2026-10-08):** P1 deferred ACK proves
  durable acceptance only. Use the original event's dedupe key/source generation
  to correlate `ops_alert_outbox`, the actual batch's `delivered_at` and native
  send ACK; compare payload hash and timing. Before an hourly batch is due,
  report queued. Missing delivery links mean UNKNOWN. Extra test messages cannot
  substitute for evidence of the original alert chain.

## 5. The source itself can be stale

- IPs taken from old rollout logs make healthy hosts appear unreachable. Verify
  the current address before a liveness verdict.
- Cross-repository assertions in comments expire unnoticed; one guided
  production for three months.
- Backlogs age: when asked what remained in `endgame-plan`, 4 items were already
  complete and 1 was hidden in a merged row. Reconcile before working.
- Of 15 self-reported visual defects, 6 were observation errors. Measure before
  changing code.
- A benchmark site with fabricated data entered long-term memory and was cited
  as measured evidence for 9 days.

## Pre-conclusion checklist

- [ ] Probe failure is distinguishable from the claimed verdict.
- [ ] Negative results have independent arrival proof and controls.
- [ ] Fleet-wide claims include a known-good host in the same batch.
- [ ] Numbers come from current authoritative sources, not old logs/comments/lists.
- [ ] Measurement and claim concern the same layer.
- [ ] Repair timers cannot silently mask the behavior being measured.
- [ ] Report inability to measure explicitly; it is a valid, necessary conclusion.
---
name: verification-before-completion
description: Use when about to claim work is complete, fixed, or passing, before committing or creating PRs - requires running verification commands and confirming output before making any success claims; evidence before assertions always
---

# Verification Before Completion

## Overview

**Core principle:** Evidence before claims, always.

**Violating the letter of this rule is violating the spirit of this rule.**

## The Iron Law

```
NO COMPLETION CLAIMS WITHOUT FRESH VERIFICATION EVIDENCE
```

If you haven't run the verification command in this message, you cannot claim it passes.

## The Gate Function

```
BEFORE claiming any status or expressing satisfaction:

1. IDENTIFY: What command proves this claim?
2. RUN: Execute the FULL command (fresh, complete)
3. READ: Full output, check exit code, count failures
4. VERIFY: Does output confirm the claim?
   - If NO: State actual status with evidence
   - If YES: State claim WITH evidence
5. ONLY THEN: Make the claim

Skip any step = lying, not verifying
```

## Common Failures

| Claim | Requires | Not Sufficient |
|-------|----------|----------------|
| Tests pass | Test command output: 0 failures | Previous run, "should pass" |
| Linter clean | Linter output: 0 errors | Partial check, extrapolation |
| Build succeeds | Build command: exit 0 | Linter passing, logs look good |
| Bug fixed | Test original symptom: passes | Code changed, assumed fixed |
| Regression test works | Red-green cycle verified | Test passes once |
| Agent completed | VCS diff shows changes | Agent reports "success" |
| Requirements met | Line-by-line checklist | Tests passing |

## Red Flags - STOP

- Using "should", "probably", "seems to"
- Expressing satisfaction before verification ("Great!", "Perfect!", "Done!", etc.)
- About to commit/push/PR without verification
- Trusting agent success reports
- Relying on partial verification
- Thinking "just this once"
- Tired and wanting work over
- **ANY wording implying success without having run verification**

## Rationalization Prevention

| Excuse | Reality |
|--------|---------|
| "Should work now" | RUN the verification |
| "I'm confident" | Confidence ≠ evidence |
| "Just this once" | No exceptions |
| "Linter passed" | Linter ≠ compiler |
| "Agent said success" | Verify independently |
| "I'm tired" | Exhaustion ≠ excuse |
| "Partial check is enough" | Partial proves nothing |
| "Different words so rule doesn't apply" | Spirit over letter |

## Key Patterns

**Tests:**
```
✅ [Run test command] [See: 34/34 pass] "All tests pass"
❌ "Should pass now" / "Looks correct"
```

**Regression tests (TDD Red-Green):**
```
✅ Write → Run (pass) → Revert fix → Run (MUST FAIL) → Restore → Run (pass)
❌ "I've written a regression test" (without red-green verification)
```

**Build:**
```
✅ [Run build] [See: exit 0] "Build passes"
❌ "Linter passed" (linter doesn't check compilation)
```

**Requirements:**
```
✅ Re-read plan → Create checklist → Verify each → Report gaps or completion
❌ "Tests pass, phase complete"
```

**Agent delegation:**
```
✅ Agent reports success → Check VCS diff → Verify changes → Report actual state
❌ Trust agent report
```

## When To Apply

**ALWAYS before:**
- ANY variation of success/completion claims
- ANY expression of satisfaction
- ANY positive statement about work state
- Committing, PR creation, task completion
- Moving to next task
- Delegating to agents

**Rule applies to:**
- Exact phrases
- Paraphrases and synonyms
- Implications of success
- ANY communication suggesting completion/correctness

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Read-only frozen source contract; this does not attest test execution."""
import argparse
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path

SPEC = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("first_native_consumer", Path(__file__).with_name("pytest-shards.py"))
assert SPEC and SPEC.loader
CONSUMER = importlib.util.module_from_spec(SPEC)
SPEC.loader.exec_module(CONSUMER)


def check(source):
    policy = CONSUMER.first_native_policy()
    revision = policy["source_revision"]
    files = CONSUMER.tree_inputs(source, revision)
    for path, blob in policy["module_blobs"].items():
        CONSUMER.require(files.get(path) == ("100644", blob), "native module mode or blob changed: " + path)
    baseline = CONSUMER.skip_baseline()
    common = CONSUMER.shared_inputs(source, revision)
    predecessor = CONSUMER.estate_successor_proof(baseline, source, revision)
    CONSUMER.admit_shared_inputs(baseline, common, files, source_revision=revision,
                                estate_successor_of=predecessor)
    return {"status": "FROZEN_SOURCE_CONTRACT_VALID", "source_revision": revision,
            "modules": len(policy["module_blobs"]), "native_case_obligations": len(policy["cases"]),
            "execution_evaluated": False, "release_acceptance": False}


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--source", required=True, type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    try:
        result = check(args.source)
    except (ValueError, OSError) as exc:
        parser.exit(1, str(exc) + "\n")
    print(json.dumps(result, sort_keys=True))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

            with self.subTest(mutation=mutation), self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "historical case"):
